empty if none survived","maxItems":32,"type":"array","items":{"type":"object","properties":{"file":{"description":"Repo-relative path of the file the finding is in","type":"string"},"line":{"description":"1-indexed line the finding anchors to","type":"integer","minimum":-9007199254740991,"maximum":9007199254740991},"summary":{"description":"One-sentence statement of the defect","type":"string"},"short_summary":{"description":"Compressed label for compact UI (≤60 chars): the claim alone, no rationale or consequence clause","type":"string","maxLength":60},"failure_scenario":{"description":"Concrete inputs/state → wrong output/crash","type":"string"},"category":{"description":"Short kebab-case slug of the finding type, e.g. \"correctness\", \"simplification\", \"efficiency\", \"test-coverage\"","type":"string","maxLength":40},"verdict":{"description":"Set when a verify pass ran; absent on inline-only reviews","type":"string","enum":["CONFIRMED","PLAUSIBLE"]},"outcome":{"description":"Set ONLY when re-reporting after applying fixes: what happened to this finding","type":"string","enum":["fixed","skipped","no_change_needed"]}},"required":["file","summary","failure_scenario"],"additionalProperties":false}}},"required":["findings"],"additionalProperties":false},"eager_input_streaming":true}},{"name":"ScheduleWakeup","description":"Schedule when to resume work in /loop dynamic mode — the user invoked /loop without an interval, asking you to self-pace iterations of a specific task.\n\nDo NOT schedule a short-interval wakeup to poll for background work you started — when harness-tracked work finishes, you are re-invoked automatically, so polling is wasted. Instead schedule a long fallback (1200s+) so the loop survives if the work hangs or never notifies. The exception is external work the harness cannot track (a CI run, a deploy, a remote queue) — there, pick a delay matched to how fast that state actually changes.\n\nPass the same /loop prompt back via `prompt` each turn so the next firing repeats the task. For an autonomous /loop (no user prompt), pass the literal sentinel `<<autonomous-loop-dynamic>>` as `prompt` instead — the runtime resolves it back to the autonomous-loop instructions at fire time. (There is a similar `<<autonomous-loop>>` sentinel for CronCreate-based autonomous loops; do not confuse the two — ScheduleWakeup always uses the `-dynamic` variant.) To end the loop, call this tool with `stop: true` (omit every other field) — the loop ends immediately and no further wakeups fire.\n\nSet `noop: true` if nothing changed — you checked and there's nothing to report (\"no change\", \"still waiting\", \"quiet hold\"). Set `noop: false` if something happened worth keeping — you edited a file, posted a message, advanced state, or surfaced a finding. Consecutive `noop: true` ticks are collapsed in the user's terminal view and tracked as a streak, so long quiet holds stay legible to the user without scrolling. Omit `noop` when stopping (`stop: true`).\n\n## Picking delaySeconds\n\nThis session's requests use a 1-hour Anthropic prompt-cache TTL, so effectively every allowed delay (the runtime clamps to [60, 3600]) wakes up with your conversation context still cached. There is no cache cliff inside that range to pace around, and scheduling extra wakeups just to keep the cache warm is pure waste — never do that. (If the session enters usage overage, later requests drop to the 5-minute TTL; don't try to track or preempt that — the guidance here stays the same.)\n\nMatch the delay to what you're actually waiting for:\n\n- **Actively polling external state the harness can't notify you about** (a CI run, a deploy, a remote queue): pick the delay from how fast that state actually changes. A CI run that takes ~8 minutes deserves one ~480s check, not eight 60s ones.\n- **The long fallback heartbeat** (something else — a Monitor, a task notification — is the primary wake signal): 1200s+, so quiet wakeups stay rare.\n- **Idle ticks with no specific signal to watch**: default to **1200s–1800s** (20–30 min). The loop still checks back regularly, and the user can always interrupt if they need you sooner.\n\nDon't think in cache windows — think about what you're actually waiting for.\n\n## The reason field\n\nOne short sentence on what you chose and why. Goes to telemetry and is shown back to the user. \"watching CI run\" beats \"waiting.\" The user reads this to understand what you're doing without having to predict your cadence in advance — make it specific.\n","schema":{"name":"ScheduleWakeup","description":"Schedule when to resume work in /loop dynamic mode — the user invoked /loop without an interval, asking you to self-pace iterations of a specific task.\n\nDo NOT schedule a short-interval wakeup to poll for background work you started — when harness-tracked work finishes, you are re-invoked automatically, so polling is wasted. {"parentUuid":"9602e6e0-f7ae-49a6-af98-d1fa5727bc84","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"1a85f451-ad79-4132-af08-92c61fabd65a","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MvvHtfdNGmPcvW2VSuy545","type":"tool_result","content":"# CONTEXT.md glossary\n\nClient: Kenyon College Varsity Baseball Team Alumni.\n\n**Head Coach (Matt Burdette)**: The site's owner. Holds the *admin* role and can edit everything, including the Team Schedule.\n\n**Second Editor (Joe)**: Holds the *editor* role. Can write and publish any content, including Alumni Events and the Alumni Network, except Locked pages such as the Team Schedule. Cannot manage users.\n\n**Team Schedule**: The current team's schedule, a single page in the public navigation. Only the Head Coach can edit it.\n\n**Alumni Events**: The public section for alumni gatherings. It is a page in the navigation that lists individual Event posts.\n\n**Post Type**: The required choice on every post: *Event* or *Team Update*. It decides where the post appears.\n\n**Event**: A post of type Event, such as a reunion or game-watch. Date and place go in the title and body. It appears on Alumni Events and the Home page.\n\n**Team Update**: A post of type Team Update, with news about the current team for fans, parents, students and alumni. It appears on the Home page.\n\n**Home**: The front page. A welcome text, the latest three Team Updates, the most recent Events, and links to Team Schedule, Alumni Events and Alumni Network. Both editors can change it.\n\n**Deactivate**: Turn off a user's account so they cannot log in. Users are never deleted, so authors and \"recorded by\" always point at a real person. The Head Coach cannot deactivate or demote himself.\n\n**Alumni Network**: The public section listing alumni who have chosen to be listed. Shows names only; emails are never published.\n\n**Listing**: One alumnus's entry in the Alumni Network. Holds name, email, optional class year, consent date, and who recorded it. Only listings with consent reach the public site.\n\n**Opt-in**: An alumnus agrees to be listed. Alumni cannot log in, so the Second Editor records the consent when the alumnus asks to be listed.\n\n**Consent date**: When an alumnus asked to be listed. Stored on the Listing.\n\n**Alumnus**: A former player on the varsity team. Never a user of the admin console.\n\n**Locked**: A page only the Head Coach may change. The Team Schedule is Locked. The Second Editor can open a Locked page to read it, but cannot edit, publish, unpublish or delete it. Only the Head Coach can lock or unlock a page.\n\n**Alumni Contact Line**: The single public line on the Alumni Network page telling visitors to email the Second Editor, who then introduces them to the alumnus. It is the only way to reach an alumnus through the site.\n\n**Page**: A content item with a title, slug and Markdown body, managed in the admin console. A published Page appears in the public navigation. Home, Team Schedule, Alumni Events, Alumni Network and About are Pages.\n\n**Published status**: A Page or post is *draft* or *published*. Setting it to published makes the item eligible for the site; it is not live until **Publish** runs. The admin buttons say \"Mark published\" / \"Mark draft\" to keep the two apart.\n\n**Publish**: The explicit step that turns published content into the public site. Edits are not live until the Head Coach (or the builder, in the demo) runs it.\nT05\nCLAUDE.md\nCONTEXT.md\nREADME.md\nSECURITY-CHECKLIST.md\napp\ncms.db\ndocs\nnotes\npyproject.toml\nscripts\nsite\ntemplates\ntests\nuv.lock","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"571940b9-2f0c-4857-8b43-f86c4ae016d6","timestamp":"2026-10-06T00:09:12.264Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"# CONTEXT.md glossary\n\nClient: Kenyon College Varsity Baseball Team Alumni.\n\n**Head Coach (Matt Burdette)**: The site's owner. echo ----; gh issue view 1 --json body -q .body 2>&1","description":"Read ticket 7 and spec bodies"}},"wireIngestContext":{"toolu_011VaY5nAZWSRSY1oFAzZmeJ":{"cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms"}},"serverClassifierRequest":"5f94c067-56e8-4b7f-8c8c-e07ed7c48be0","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011Cfk1dhqSubE136fXC6aCK","attributionSkill":"mattpocock-skills:implement","attributionPlugin":"mattpocock-skills","type":"assistant","uuid":"e2f49767-04dc-4127-bc1a-ab84587ba744","timestamp":"2026-10-06T00:09:17.597Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"9d3ee875-b856-4c5f-8263-d5e667d38523","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"48eb66fe-d933-4944-9cad-403a5701513d","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"e2f49767-04dc-4127-bc1a-ab84587ba744","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"1a85f451-ad79-4132-af08-92c61fabd65a","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011VaY5nAZWSRSY1oFAzZmeJ","type":"tool_result","content":"T06: Matt can manage users\n## Parent\n\nSpec: #1\n\n## What to build\n\nMatt can manage users on an admin-only Users screen: create a user, change a role, deactivate and reactivate. A deactivated user cannot log in. Users are never deleted, and Matt cannot deactivate or demote himself, so the site is never left without an admin. Joe is refused the screen. This is capability 6.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] An admin can create a user with name, email, password and role (admin or editor); the password is stored as an argon2 hash.\n- [ ] An admin can change a user's role.\n- [ ] An admin can deactivate and reactivate a user.\n- [ ] A deactivated user is refused at login with a clear message, and an already-open session is no longer accepted.\n- [ ] There is no way to delete a user.\n- [ ] An admin cannot deactivate or demote their own account; the attempt shows an error.\n- [ ] An editor gets 403 or a redirect on every Users route, and an anonymous visitor is redirected to login.\n- [ ] Every Users form carries a CSRF token and rejects a POST without one.\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- #2 (T01)\n\n----\n## Problem Statement\n\nKenyon College Varsity Baseball Team Alumni have no website of their own. Alumni, players, fans, students and parents learn about the team, its schedule and alumni gatherings only by talking to each other. The site is run by the Head Coach (Matt Burdette), who is comfortable using websites but cannot build or edit one, and by a Second Editor (Joe), who looks after the alumni side. Matt must be able to change anything. Joe must never be able to change the Team Schedule. Nobody else may ever edit the site. Alumni who want to be listed must be able to choose it, and their emails must not be exposed to the public internet.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS in two parts, as set by ADR-001 and ADR-002. A private admin console, run on Matt's laptop (the builder's laptop in the demo), lets Matt and Joe log in and manage pages, posts, alumni listings and users. A `cms publish` step renders only the published content into plain, relative-path HTML in `site/`, which is deployed to GitHub Pages.\n\nThe public site has these pages in its navigation: **Home**, **Team Schedule**, **Alumni Events** and **Alumni Network**, plus **About**. Events and Team Updates are posts. Alumni Network shows consented alumni names only, and tells visitors to email Joe to reach an alumnus (ADR-003).\n\n### The 7 required capabilities, in this client's words\n\n1. **Login** - Matt and Joe sign in and out. Passwords are stored as argon2 hashes. A wrong password is refused with a message.\n2. **Two roles** - *admin* (Matt) can do everything. *editor* (Joe) can write and publish any content except **Locked** pages, and cannot manage users.\n3. **Posts** - Events and Team Updates. Create, read, update and delete, with title, slug, Markdown body, draft/published status, Post Type, author and timestamps.\n4. **Pages** - Home, Team Schedule, Alumni Events, Alumni Network, About and any others. Same fields and operations as posts, and a published page appears in the public navigation. The Team Schedule is Locked.\n5. **Admin console** - a dashboard with counts, a content list filterable by status and type, and an editor with a Markdown preview.\n6. **User management** - admin only: create users, change roles, deactivate and reactivate. A deactivated user cannot log in.\n7. **Public site and publish** - `cms publish` writes `site/` with published content only, sanitized Markdown, a navigation built from published pages, and relative paths.\n\n**Listings** are an extension beside these seven, not a replacement. They reuse the CRUD pattern of capability 3 and feed the export in capability 7. They exist because opt-in consent needs its own fields (see ADR-003).\n\n## User Stories\n\n1. As the Head Coach, I want to log in with my email and password, so that only I and Joe can change the site.\n2. As the Head Coach, I want a wrong password to be refused with a clear message, so that I know to try again.\n3. As the Head Coach, I want to log out, so that nobody else at a shared computer can use my session.\n4. As the Head Coach, I want my password stored only as a hash, so that it cannot be read from the database.\n5. As the Second Editor, I want to log in and out like Matt, so that my edits are attributed to me.\n6. As a visitor who is not logged in, I want to be sent to the login page when I try to open an admin address, so that I cannot see or change anything.\n7. As the Head Coach, I want to edit the Team Schedule, so that fans and alumni see the current schedule.\n8. As the Head Coach, I want the Team Schedule to be Locked, so that only I can change it.\n9. As the Second Editor, I want to open the Team Schedule and read it, so that I can see what the public sees.\n10. As the Second Editor, I want to be refused, with a clear message, when I try to edit, publish, unpublish or delete the Team Schedule, so that I know it is not mine to change.\n11. As the Head Coach, I want to lock or unlock any page, so that I can protect other pages if I need to.\n12. As the Second Editor, I want not to see lock or unlock controls, so that I cannot unlock the Team Schedule myself.\n13. As the Head Coach, I want to see counts of posts, pages, drafts and listings on a dashboard, so that I know the state of the site at a glance.\n14. As an editor, I want to filter the content list by status (draft or published) and by type, so that I can find what I need quickly.\n15. As an editor, I want to write a post in Markdown and see a preview before saving, so that I can check how it will look.\n16. As an editor, I want to choose a Post Type of Event or Team Update on every post, so that it appears in the right place.\n17. As the Second Editor, I want new posts to default to Event, so that my usual work needs one fewer click.\n18. As an editor, I want a slug generated from the title that I can change, so that I never have to invent a web address.\n19. As an editor, I want to save a post as a draft, so that I can finish it later without the public seeing it.\n20. As an editor, I want to publish and unpublish a post, so that I control what is public.\n21. As an editor, I want to delete a post, so that mistakes can be removed.\n22. As an editor, I want to see a success or error message after every save, so that I know it worked.\n23. As an editor, I want to see who wrote a post and when it was created and last updated, so that I can tell who changed what.\n24. As the Second Editor, I want to publish my Events and Alumni Network content without the Head Coach's approval, so that alumni news goes out promptly.\n25. As an editor, I want to create, edit, publish, unpublish and delete pages, so that the site's sections stay current.\n26. As a visitor, I want published pages to appear in the site's navigation, so that I can find each section.\n27. As an editor, I want an unpublished page to be missing from the navigation, so that unfinished sections are not linked.\n28. As the Second Editor, I want to add an alumnus as a Listing with name, email, optional class year and a consent date, so that only alumni who asked are on the list.\n29. As the Second Editor, I want each Listing to record who entered it and when the alumnus consented, so that there is a record of the opt-in.\n30. As the Second Editor, I want to remove a Listing or withdraw its consent, so that a name disappears at the next publish.\n31. As the Second Editor, I want a Listing without consent never to be published, so that nobody is listed by mistake.\n32. As an alumnus, I want my email never to appear on the public site, so that I am not exposed to spam.\n33. As a visitor, I want to see an alphabetical list of the alumni who chose to be listed, so that I can see who is in the network.\n34. As a visitor, I want a single line on the Alumni Network page telling me to email Joe to reach an alumnus, so that I know how to get in touch.\n35. As a visitor, I want the Alumni Events page to list Event posts newest first, so that I can see what is coming up.\n36. As a fan, I want the Home page to show a welcome, the latest three Team Updates and recent Events, so that I see news first.\n37. As a visitor, I want links from the Home page to Team Schedule, Alumni Events and Alumni Network, so that I can reach each section quickly.\n38. As a visitor on a phone, I want every public page to fit a narrow screen without sideways scrolling, so that I can read it anywhere.\n39. As the Head Coach, I want to create a user and give them the editor or admin role, so that I can add a helper.\n40. As the Head Coach, I want to change a user's role, so that I can adjust what they may do.\n41. As the Head Coach, I want to deactivate a user, so that a former helper can no longer log in.\n42. As the Head Coach, I want to reactivate a user, so that returning helpers get their access back.\n43. As the Head Coach, I want users never to be deleted, so that authors and \"recorded by\" always point to a real person.\n44. As the Head Coach, I want to be unable to deactivate or demote myself, so that the site can never be left with no admin.\n45. As the Second Editor, I want to be refused the Users screen, so that only the Head Coach manages accounts.\n46. As a deactivated user, I want to be refused at login with a message, so that I understand why.\n47. As the Head Coach, I want every form to carry a CSRF token, so that another website cannot change content through my session.\n48. As an editor, I want any `<script>` or `onerror=` in my Markdown stripped, in the preview and on the public site, so that nobody is attacked through the content.\n49. As the Head Coach, I want `cms publish` to write only published content, so that drafts never leave the database.\n50. As the Head Coach, I want published pages to use relative paths, so that the site works from the GitHub Pages subfolder.\n51. As the Head Coach, I want edits to stay private until I run publish, so that I choose when the public sees a change.\n52. As a grader, I want a seed script that fills the database with demo data from `.env.example` values, so that the app can be walked through from a clean clone.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n- **Two seams.** Behaviour is exposed through the HTTP application and through the publish export. Nothing else is a public interface.\n- **Content model.** Three content types: Page, Post and Listing. Plus Users.\n  - Pages and Posts share title, slug, Markdown body, status (draft or published), author and timestamps.\n  - Posts add a required Post Type: Event or Team Update.\n  - Pages add a Locked flag, changeable only by an admin.\n  - Listings carry name, email, optional class year, consent date, and recorded-by.\n- **Roles.** Two roles: admin and editor. A single permission check guards every route.\n  - Editors cannot reach user management.\n  - Editors can open a Locked page but cannot edit, publish, unpublish or delete it.\n  - Anonymous visitors are redirected to login.\n- **Users.** Users are deactivated, never deleted. A deactivated user cannot log in. An admin cannot deactivate or demote themselves.\n- **Login and sessions.** Passwords hashed with argon2. Signed session cookies. The login form takes email and password. Secrets are read from the environment.\n- **CSRF.** Every state-changing form carries a token, and a POST without a valid token is rejected.\n- **Markdown.** Rendered then sanitized before it is shown anywhere, including the admin preview, per the CLAUDE.md hard constraints.\n- **Admin console.** A dashboard of counts, a content list filterable by status and type, an editor with a preview, a Listings screen and a Users screen. Persistent navigation and a success or error message after each save.\n- **Special pages.** The exporter gives the Home, Alumni Events and Alumni Network pages their generated sections: recent posts, the Event list, and the consented names. The page's own Markdown is rendered above them.\n- **Alumni data.** The exporter never reads the Listing email field (ADR-003). Only consented Listings are exported, names only, in alphabetical order.\n- **Publish.** `cms publish` renders published Pages, Posts and consented Listings into `site/` with relative paths only. `site/` is generated and never edited by hand. Drafts, unconsented Listings and emails are never exported.\n- **Deployment.** `cms deploy` pushes `site/` to the `gh-pages` branch. The admin console is never deployed (ADR-001).\n- **Demo data.** The seed script creates Matt as admin, Joe as editor, the five pages with Team Schedule Locked, sample posts of both types, and sample Listings.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- **What makes a good test.** It asserts external behaviour only: an HTTP status, a redirect, a message, a file's content. It does not reach into internals.\n- **HTTP seam (most tests).** Use the existing `client` and `client_as(role)` fixtures and the app factory. Cover login and logout, roles, Locked pages, Post, Page and Listing CRUD, user management and deactivation, the content list filters, and the preview.\n  - **Persistence.** Post and Page CRUD must survive a restart: build a fresh app on the same database file and check the content is still there.\n  - **Access control.** Enumerate the admin-only routes from the app's own router, and assert an editor gets 403 or a redirect and an anonymous visitor is redirected to login, so a route added later cannot escape the check.\n  - **CSRF.** Replay each state-changing POST without a token and assert rejection, one per form.\n  - **Password hashing.** Read the stored hash and assert it is an argon2 hash, never plain text.\n- **Publish seam.** Run the export into a temporary directory and assert on the files:\n  - Drafts and unconsented Listings are absent.\n  - No alumnus email appears anywhere in the output.\n  - Every path is relative, with no `href=\"/` or `src=\"/`, and every internal link resolves.\n  - A `<script>` tag and an `onerror=` attribute are stripped.\n  - The navigation is built from published pages only, and Home, Alumni Events and the Alumni Network list are generated correctly.\n- **Seed script.** A test runs it from `.env.example` values and checks it completes with no manual steps.\n- **Prior art.** The existing walking-skeleton test and the `client_as` helper, whose docstring already shows the access-control pattern.\n- **Traceability.** Each ticket's acceptance criteria should be named by at least one test.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- Alumni accounts or logins, because the public site is static.\n- Comments.\n- Media uploads.\n- A public sign-up form for alumni.\n- A hosted admin console on the internet.\n- RSVPs, because a static site cannot take submissions.\n- Scheduled or recurring events.\n- A structured game and results database. The Team Schedule is a hand-edited Markdown table.\n- Search, including a name search on the Alumni Network.\n- Multi-site, drag-and-drop page builders and rich-text WYSIWYG editors, as the manual excludes them.\n\n## Further Notes\n\n- **Decisions made in the grill:** the content model and page names, Joe's role and Locked pages, consent and email privacy, the Alumni Contact Line, publish operator, home page, user rules, and ADR-003.\n- **Defaults not decided in the grill. Please confirm or correct:**\n  - Slugs are generated from the title and editable.\n  - Navigation shows Home first, then the other published pages alphabetically.\n  - Home shows three Team Updates and three recent Events.\n  - Home, Alumni Events and Alumni Network are recognised by fixed slugs for their generated sections.\n  - Joe's public contact address is a site setting, not part of a Listing.\n- **Real-world operation.** Matt is the intended operator of the admin console and `cms publish`. In the demo the builder's laptop plays Matt's. Published withdrawals of a name cannot recall a page already cached by GitHub Pages.\n- **Vocabulary.** Terms follow `CONTEXT.md`. ADR-001, ADR-002 and ADR-003 apply.","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"c53d921a-a48d-4916-a105-a22bd47a6925","timestamp":"2026-10-06T00:09:19.051Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"T06: Matt can manage users\n## Parent\n\nSpec: #1\n\n## What to build\n\nMatt can manage users on an admin-only Users screen: create a user, change a role, deactivate and reactivate. A deactivated user cannot log in. Users are never deleted, and Matt cannot deactivate or demote himself, so the site is never left without an admin. Joe is refused the screen. This is capability 6.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] An admin can create a user with name, email, password and role (admin or editor); the password is stored as an argon2 hash.\n- [ ] An admin can change a user's role.\n- [ ] An admin can deactivate and reactivate a user.\n- [ ] A deactivated user is refused at login with a clear message, and an already-open session is no longer accepted.\n- [ ] There is no way to delete a user.\n- [ ] An admin cannot deactivate or demote their own account; the attempt shows an error.\n- [ ] An editor gets 403 or a redirect on every Users route, and an anonymous visitor is redirected to login.\n- [ ] Every Users form carries a CSRF token and rejects a POST without one.\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- #2 (T01)\n\n----\n## Problem Statement\n\nKenyon College Varsity Baseball Team Alumni have no website of their own. Alumni, players, fans, students and parents learn about the team, its schedule and alumni gatherings only by talking to each other. The site is run by the Head Coach (Matt Burdette), who is comfortable using websites but cannot build or edit one, and by a Second Editor (Joe), who looks after the alumni side. Matt must be able to change anything. Joe must never be able to change the Team Schedule. Nobody else may ever edit the site. Alumni who want to be listed must be able to choose it, and their emails must not be exposed to the public internet.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS in two parts, as set by ADR-001 and ADR-002. A private admin console, run on Matt's laptop (the builder's laptop in the demo), lets Matt and Joe log in and manage pages, posts, alumni listings and users. A `cms publish` step renders only the published content into plain, relative-path HTML in `site/`, which is deployed to GitHub Pages.\n\nThe public site has these pages in its navigation: **Home**, **Team Schedule**, **Alumni Events** and **Alumni Network**, plus **About**. Events and Team Updates are posts. Alumni Network shows consented alumni names only, and tells visitors to email Joe to reach an alumnus (ADR-003).\n\n### The 7 required capabilities, in this client's words\n\n1. **Login** - Matt and Joe sign in and out. Passwords are stored as argon2 hashes. A wrong password is refused with a message.\n2. **Two roles** - *admin* (Matt) can do everything. *editor* (Joe) can write and publish any content except **Locked** pages, and cannot manage users.\n3. **Posts** - Events and Team Updates. Create, read, update and delete, with title, slug, Markdown body, draft/published status, Post Type, author and timestamps.\n4. **Pages** - Home, Team Schedule, Alumni Events, Alumni Network, About and any others. Same fields and operations as posts, and a published page appears in the public navigation. The Team Schedule is Locked.\n5. **Admin console** - a dashboard with counts, a content list filterable by status and type, and an editor with a Markdown preview.\n6. **User management** - admin only: create users, change roles, deactivate and reactivate. A deactivated user cannot log in.\n7. **Public site and publish** - `cms publish` writes `site/` with published content only, sanitized Markdown, a navigation built from published pages, and relative paths.\n\n**Listings** are an extension beside these seven, not a replacement. They reuse the CRUD pattern of capability 3 and feed the export in capability 7. They exist because opt-in consent needs its own fields (see ADR-003).\n\n## User Stories\n\n1. As the Head Coach, I want to log in with my email and password, so that only I and Joe can change the site.\n2. As the Head Coach, I want a wrong password to be refused with a clear message, so that I know to try again.\n3. As the Head Coach, I want to log out, so that nobody else at a shared computer can use my session.\n4. As the Head Coach, I want my password stored only as a hash, so that it cannot be read from the database.\n5. As the Second Editor, I want to log in and out like Matt, so that my edits are attributed to me.\n6. As a visitor who is not logged in, I want to be sent to the login page when I try to open an admin address, so that I cannot see or change anything.\n7. As the Head Coach, I want to edit the Team Schedule, so that fans and alumni see the current schedule.\n8. As the Head Coach, I want the Team Schedule to be Locked, so that only I can change it.\n9. {"parentUuid":"97d0cd1d-f908-4ce3-a476-4b6b65bb3b13","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"1a85f451-ad79-4132-af08-92c61fabd65a","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ENeW6qjus1oW8aLPrDca7C","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Users and passwords. Passwords are stored only as argon2 hashes.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError\n\nROLES = (\"admin\", \"editor\")\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n# Verified against when the email is unknown, so an unknown account and a wrong\n# password take about the same time.\n_DUMMY_HASH = _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")\n\n\ndef hash_password(password: str) -> str:\n    return _hasher.hash(password)\n\n\ndef verify_password(password_hash: str, password: str) -> bool:\n    try:\n        return _hasher.verify(password_hash, password)\n    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):\n        return False\n\n\ndef create_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, email: str, name: str,\n                password: str, role: str) -> int:\n    if role not in ROLES:\n        raise ValueError(f\"unknown role: {role}\")\n    cur = conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO users (email, name, password_hash, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (email.strip(), name, hash_password(password), role),\n    )\n    conn.commit()\n    assert cur.lastrowid is not None\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef get_by_email(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (email.strip(),)\n    ).fetchone()\n\n\ndef get_by_id(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef authenticate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str, password: str\n                 ) -> tuple[sqlite3.Row | None, str | None]:\n    \"\"\"Return (user, None) on success, or (None, reason).\n\n    reason is \"invalid\" for an unknown email or wrong password (the caller must\n    show one message for both), or \"inactive\" when the password was right but\n    the account is deactivated.\n    \"\"\"\n    user = get_by_email(conn, email)\n    if user is None:\n        verify_password(_DUMMY_HASH, password)\n        return None, \"invalid\"\n    if not verify_password(user[\"password_hash\"], password):\n        return None, \"invalid\"\n    if not user[\"active\"]:\n        return None, \"inactive\"\n    return user, None\n\"\"\"Shared web plumbing: templates, sessions, the login check and CSRF.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hmac\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import db, settings, users\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\nclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised for an anonymous visitor; the app turns it into a redirect.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    conn = db.connect(request.app.state.database_path)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request, conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    \"\"\"The signed-in, active user, or LoginRequired. Guards every admin route.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = users.get_by_id(conn, user_id) if user_id else None\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise LoginRequired()\n    return user\n\n\nclass Refused(Exception):\n    \"\"\"The signed-in user may not do this; the app turns it into the refusal page.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, message: str = \"Only the Head Coach can do this.\"):\n        super().__init__(message)\n        self.message = message\n\n\ndef require_admin(user=Depends(current_user)):\n    \"\"\"Dependency for admin-only routes: the signed-in user must be an admin.\"\"\"\n    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        raise Refused()\n    return user\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def verify_csrf(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Dependency for every state-changing form: reject a missing/bad token.\"\"\"\n    form = await request.form()\n    sent = form.get(\"csrf_token\")\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if (not isinstance(sent, str) or not expected\n            or not hmac.compare_digest(sent, expected)):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Missing or invalid CSRF token.\")\n\n\ndef flash(request: Request, text: str, kind: str = \"ok\") -> None:\n    \"\"\"Queue a message to show on the next page (success \"ok\" or \"error\").\"\"\"\n    request.session[\"flash\"] = {\"kind\": kind, \"text\": text}\n\n\ndef render(request: Request, name: str, context: dict | None = None,\n           status_code: int = 200, user=None):\n    ctx = {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request),\n           \"user\": user, \"flash\": request.session.pop(\"flash\", None)}\n    ctx.update(context or {})\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, name, ctx, status_code=status_code)\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nThe admin console answers under /admin (behind login) and the public site\npreview answers at /. Routes live in their own modules under app/routes and are\nincluded here. \"message\": message},\n                  status_code=403, user=user or _session_user(request))\n\n\ndef _session_user(request: Request):\n    conn = db.connect(request.app.state.database_path)\n    try:\n        user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n        return users.get_by_id(conn, user_id) if user_id else None\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef create_app(database_path: Path | str | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\")\n    app.state.database_path = database_path or settings.DATABASE_PATH\n    db.init_db(app.state.database_path)\n\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       session_cookie=\"cms_session\", same_site=\"lax\",\n                       https_only=False, max_age=60 * 60 * 12)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(LoginRequired)\n    def _login_required(request: Request, exc: LoginRequired):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(Refused)\n    def _refused(request: Request, exc: Refused):\n        return _refusal(request, message=exc.message)\n\n    app.include_router(auth.router)\n One connection per request; the schema is created on startup.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n    email         TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    name          TEXT NOT NULL,\n    password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n    role          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    created_at    TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pages (\n    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n    title      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    slug       TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    body       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    locked     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    author_id  INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),\n    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n    title      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    slug       TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    body       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    post_type  TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (post_type IN ('event', 'team_update')),\n    author_id  INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),\n    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path | str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path | str) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    try:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n        # Databases made before T05 have no Locked flag on pages.\n        columns = [r[\"name\"] for r in conn.execute(\"PRAGMA table_info(pages)\")]\n        if \"locked\" not in columns:\n            conn.execute(\"ALTER TABLE pages ADD COLUMN locked INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0\")\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import db, users\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. ((\"admin\", \"Matt Burdette\"), (\"editor\", \"Joe\")):\n        users.create_user(conn, email=DEMO_USERS[role][\"email\"], name=name,\n                          password=DEMO_USERS[role][\"password\"], role=role)\n    conn.close()\n    return path\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(database_path) -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app(database_path))\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(database_path):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app(database_path))\n        token = _csrf_from(c.get(\"/login\"))\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"],\n                                          \"csrf_token\": token},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        if response.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        assert response.status_code in (200, sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import pages\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.web import Refused, current_user, flash, get_db, render, require_admin, verify_csrf\n\n# Every route here needs a signed-in user; every POST also needs a CSRF token.\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/pages\", dependencies=[Depends(current_user)])\ncsrf_required = [Depends(verify_csrf)]\n\n\nLOCKED_MESSAGE = (\"This page is Locked. Only the Head Coach can change it: you can read it, \"\n                  \"but not edit, publish, unpublish or delete it.\")\n\n\ndef _page_or_404(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    page = pages.get(conn, page_id)\n    if page is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No such page.\")\n    return page\n\n\ndef _changeable_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int, user) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    \"\"\"The page, if this user may change it; Locked pages are for admins only.\"\"\"\n    page = _page_or_404(conn, page_id)\n    if page[\"locked\"] and user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        raise Refused(LOCKED_MESSAGE)\n    return page\n\n\ndef _back_to_editor(page_id: int) -> RedirectResponse:\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}/edit\", status_code=303)\n\n\ndef _editor(request: Request, user, form: dict, page: sqlite3.Row | None = None,\n            status_code: int = 200, preview: str | None = None, error: str | None = None):\n    if error:\n        flash(request, error, \"error\")\n    return render(request, \"admin/page_edit.html\",\n                  {\"title\": \"Edit page\" if page else \"New page\", \"form\": form,\n                   \"page\": page, \"preview\": preview}, status_code=status_code, user=user)\n\n\ndef _slug_taken(fields: dict) -> str:\n    return f\"The slug “{fields['slug']}” is already used by another page.\"\n\n\ndef _clean(title: str, slug: str, body: str, status: str) -> tuple[dict, str | None]:\n    \"\"\"Normalise the submitted fields; return (fields, error).\"\"\"\n    title = title.strip()\n    fields = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": pages.slugify(slug or title), \"body\": body,\n              \"status\": status if status in pages.STATUSES else \"draft\"}\n    if not title:\n        return fields, \"A page needs a title.\"\n\"\"\"T05: only Matt can change Locked pages. Each test names the criterion it covers.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.routing import APIRoute\n\nfrom app import db, pages\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom app.web import require_admin\nfrom tests.conftest import _csrf_from\n\n\ndef _seed_page(database_path, title=\"Team Schedule\", slug=\"team-schedule\", locked=True):\n    conn = db.connect(database_path)\n    try:\n        page_id = pages.create(conn, title=title, slug=slug, body=\"Sat 1pm vs Denison\",\n                               status=\"published\", author_id=1)\n        if locked:\n            pages.set_locked(conn, page_id, True)\n        return page_id\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef _token(c):\n    return _csrf_from(c.get(\"/admin/pages/new\"))\n\n\ndef _save(c, page_id, **over):\n    data = {\"title\": \"Changed\", \"slug\": \"team-schedule\", \"body\": \"x\",\n            \"status\": \"published\", \"csrf_token\": _token(c)}\n    data.update(over)\n    return c.post(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef _action(c, page_id, action):\n    return c.post(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}/{action}\", data={\"csrf_token\": _token(c)},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_seeded_team_schedule_is_locked(tmp_path, monkeypatch):\n    \"\"\"The seeded Team Schedule page is Locked.\"\"\"\n    from scripts import seed_demo\n    monkeypatch.setattr(\"app.settings.DATABASE_PATH\", tmp_path / \"seed.db\")\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\", \"a-pw\")\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\", \"e-pw\")\n    assert seed_demo.main() == 0\n    conn = db.connect(tmp_path / \"seed.db\")\n    row = conn.execute(\"SELECT locked FROM pages WHERE slug = 'team-schedule'\").fetchone()\n    conn.close()","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"324c8665-53d1-4a84-b781-474405bdba2b","timestamp":"2026-10-06T00:09:23.470Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"\"\"\"Users and passwords. Passwords are stored only as argon2 hashes.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError\n\nROLES = (\"admin\", \"editor\")\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n# Verified against when the email is unknown, so an unknown account and a wrong\n# password take about the same time.\n_DUMMY_HASH = _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")\n\n\ndef hash_password(password: str) -> str:\n    return _hasher.hash(password)\n\n\ndef verify_password(password_hash: str, password: str) -> bool:\n    try:\n        return _hasher.verify(password_hash, password)\n    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):\n        return False\n\n\ndef create_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, email: str, name: str,\n                password: str, role: str) -> int:\n    if role not in ROLES:\n        raise ValueError(f\"unknown role: {role}\")\n    cur = conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO users (email, name, password_hash, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (email.strip(), name, hash_password(password), role),\n    )\n    conn.commit()\n    assert cur.lastrowid is not None\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef get_by_email(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (email.strip(),)\n    ).fetchone()\n\n\ndef get_by_id(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef authenticate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str, password: str\n                 ) -> tuple[sqlite3.Row | None, str | None]:\n    \"\"\"Return (user, None) on success, or (None, reason).\n\n    reason is \"invalid\" for an unknown email or wrong password (the caller must\n    show one message for both), or \"inactive\" when the password was right but\n    the account is deactivated.\n    \"\"\"\n    user = get_by_email(conn, email)\n    if user is None:\n        verify_password(_DUMMY_HASH, password)\n        return None, \"invalid\"\n    if not verify_password(user[\"password_hash\"], password):\n        return None, \"invalid\"\n    if not user[\"active\"]:\n        return None, \"inactive\"\n    return user, None\n\"\"\"Shared web plumbing: templates, sessions, the login check and CSRF.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hmac\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import db, settings, users\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\nclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised for an anonymous visitor; the app turns it into a redirect.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    conn = db.connect(request.app.state.database_path)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request, conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    \"\"\"The signed-in, active user, or LoginRequired. Guards every admin route.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = users.get_by_id(conn, user_id) if user_id else None\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise LoginRequired()\n    return user\n\n\nclass Refused(Exception):\n    \"\"\"The signed-in user may not do this; the app turns it into the refusal page.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, message: str = \"Only the Head Coach can do this.\"):\n        super().__init__(message)\n        self.message = message\n\n\ndef require_admin(user=Depends(current_user)):\n    \"\"\"Dependency for admin-only routes: the signed-in user must be an admin.\"\"\"\n    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        raise Refused()\n    return user\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def verify_csrf(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Dependency for every state-changing form: reject a missing/bad token.\"\"\"\n    form = await request.form()\n    sent = form.get(\"csrf_token\")\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if (not isinstance(sent, str) or not expected\n            or not hmac.compare_digest(sent, expected)):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Missing or invalid CSRF token.\")\n\n\ndef flash(request: Request, text: str, kind: str = \"ok\") -> None:\n    \"\"\"Queue a message to show on the next page (success \"ok\" or \"error\").\"\"\"\n    request.session[\"flash\"] = {\"kind\": kind, \"text\": text}\n\n\ndef render(request: Request, name: str, context: dict | None = None,\n           status_code: int = 200, user=None):\n    ctx = {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request),\n           \"user\": user, \"flash\": request.session.pop(\"flash\", None)}\n    ctx.update(context or {})\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, name, ctx, status_code=status_code)\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nThe admin console answers under /admin (behind login) and the public site\npreview answers at /. Routes live in their own modules under app/routes and are\nincluded here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import db, settings, users\nfrom app.routes import auth, content, pages, posts\nfrom app.web import LoginRequired, Refused, current_user, render, templates\n\n\ndef _refusal(request: Request, user=None, message: str = Refused().message):\n    return render(request, \"admin/refused.html\", {\"title\": \"Not allowed\", \"message\": message},\n                  status_code=403, user=user or _session_user(request))\n\n\ndef _session_user(request: Request):\n    conn = db.connect(request.app.state.database_path)\n    try:\n        user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n        return users.get_by_id(conn, user_id) if user_id else None\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef create_app(database_path: Path | str | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\")\n    app.state.database_path = database_path or settings.DATABASE_PATH\n    db.init_db(app.state.database_path)\n\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       session_cookie=\"cms_session\", same_site=\"lax\",\n                       https_only=False, max_age=60 * 60 * 12)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(LoginRequired)\n    def _login_required(request: Request, exc: LoginRequired):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(Refused)\n    def _refused(request: Request, exc: Refused):\n        return _refusal(request, message=exc.message)\n\n    app.include_router(auth.router)\n    app.include_router(pages.router)\n    app.include_router(posts.router)\n    app.include_router(content.router)\n\n    @app.get(\"/admin\")\n    def admin_home(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n        return render(request, \"admin/hello.html\", {\"title\": \"Admin\"}, user=user)\n\n    @app.get(\"/admin/refused\")\n    def refused_page(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n        return _refusal(request, user)\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n\"\"\"Login and logout.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import users\nfrom app.web import get_db, render, verify_csrf\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\n\nINVALID = \"That email and password do not match. Please try again.\"\nINACTIVE = \"This account has been deactivated. Ask the Head Coach to reactivate it.\"\n\n\n@router.get(\"/login\")\ndef login_form(request: Request):\n    if request.session.get(\"user_id\"):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/admin\", status_code=303)\n    return render(request, \"admin/login.html\", {\"title\": \"Sign in\"})\n\n\n@router.post(\"/login\", dependencies=[Depends(verify_csrf)])\ndef login(request: Request, email: str = Form(\"\"), password: str = Form(\"\"),\n          conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    user, reason = users.authenticate(conn, email, password)\n    if user is None:\n        message = INACTIVE if reason == \"inactive\" else INVALID\n        return render(request, \"admin/login.html\",\n                      {\"title\": \"Sign in\", \"error\": message, \"email\": email},\n                      status_code=401)\n    # A fresh session on login (new CSRF token too) prevents session fixation.\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n    request.session[\"role\"] = user[\"role\"]\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/logout\", dependencies=[Depends(verify_csrf)])\ndef logout(request: Request):\n    request.session.clear()\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\"\"\"SQLite access. One connection per request; the schema is created on startup.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n    email         TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    name          TEXT NOT NULL,\n    password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n    role          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    created_at    TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pages (\n    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n    title      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    slug       TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    body       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    locked     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    author_id  INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),\n    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n    title      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    slug       TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    body       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    post_type  TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (post_type IN ('event', 'team_update')),\n    author_id  INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),\n    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path | str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path | str) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    try:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n        # Databases made before T05 have no Locked flag on pages.\n        columns = [r[\"name\"] for r in conn.execute(\"PRAGMA table_info(pages)\")]\n        if \"locked\" not in columns:\n            conn.execute(\"ALTER TABLE pages ADD COLUMN locked INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0\")\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import db, users\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\ndef _csrf_from(response) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Pull the CSRF token out of a rendered form.\"\"\"\n    match = re.search(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"', response.text)\n    assert match, \"page has no CSRF token\"\n    return match.group(1)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef database_path(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"A fresh SQLite file with the two demo users, so tests never share state.\"\"\"\n    path = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n    db.init_db(path)\n    conn = db.connect(path)\n    for role, name in ((\"admin\", \"Matt Burdette\"), (\"editor\", \"Joe\")):\n        users.create_user(conn, email=DEMO_USERS[role][\"email\"], name=name,\n                          password=DEMO_USERS[role][\"password\"], role=role)\n    conn.close()\n    return path\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(database_path) -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app(database_path))\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(database_path):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app(database_path))\n        token = _csrf_from(c.get(\"/login\"))\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"],\n                                          \"csrf_token\": token},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        if response.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %} · Admin</title>\n  <style>\n    body { font-family: system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0; color: #222; }\n    header { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;\n             gap: 1rem; padding: .75rem 1rem; background: #4b2e83; color: #fff; }\n    header a, header button { color: #fff; }\n    header form { display: inline; margin: 0; }\n    header button { background: none; border: 1px solid #fff; border-radius: 4px;\n                    padding: .25rem .6rem; cursor: pointer; }\n    main { max-width: 52rem; margin: 1.5rem auto; padding: 0 1rem; }\n    label { display: block; margin: .75rem 0 .25rem; }\n    textarea { width: 100%; padding: .5rem; box-sizing: border-box; font: inherit; }\n    table { border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; }\n    th, td { text-align: left; padding: .4rem; border-bottom: 1px solid #ddd; }\n    td form { display: inline; }\n    #preview { border: 1px solid #ccc; padding: 0 1rem; margin-top: 1rem; }\n    input:not([type=hidden]) { width: 100%; padding: .5rem; box-sizing: border-box; }\n    .msg-error { background: #fde8e8; border: 1px solid #c53030; padding: .6rem; }\n    .msg-ok { background: #e6f4ea; border: 1px solid #2f855a; padding: .6rem; }\n  </style>\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header>\n    <a href=\"/admin\">Kenyon Baseball Alumni · Admin</a>\n    {% if user %}<nav><a href=\"/admin/content\">Content</a> · <a href=\"/admin/pages\">Pages</a> · <a href=\"/admin/posts/new\">New post</a></nav>{% endif %}\n    {% if user %}\n    <form method=\"post\" action=\"/logout\">\n      <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n      <span>Signed in as {{ user[\"name\"] }} ({{ user[\"role\"] }})</span>\n      <button type=\"submit\">Sign out</button>\n    </form>\n    {% endif %}\n  </header>\n  <main>\n  {% if flash %}<p class=\"msg-{{ flash.kind }}\" role=\"{{ 'alert' if flash.kind == 'error' else 'status' }}\">{{ flash.text }}</p>{% endif %}\n  {% block content %}{% endblock %}\n  </main>\n</body>\n</html>\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Sign in</h1>\n  {% if error %}<p class=\"msg-error\" role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/login\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <label for=\"email\">Email</label>\n    <input id=\"email\" name=\"email\" type=\"email\" value=\"{{ email or '' }}\" required autofocus>\n    <label for=\"password\">Password</label>\n    <input id=\"password\" name=\"password\" type=\"password\" required>\n    <p><button type=\"submit\">Sign in</button></p>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Pages</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/pages/new\">New page</a></p>\n  {% if pages %}\n  <table>\n    <thead><tr><th>Title</th><th>Status</th><th>Last update</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>\n    <tbody>\n    {% for p in pages %}\n      <tr>\n        <td><a href=\"/admin/pages/{{ p.id }}/edit\">{{ p.title }}</a>{% if p.locked %} <strong>(Locked)</strong>{% endif %}</td>\n        <td>{{ p.status }}</td>\n        <td>{{ p.updated_at }}</td>\n        <td>\n          {% if p.locked and user[\"role\"] != \"admin\" %}\n          <em>Only the Head Coach can change this page.</em>\n          {% else %}\n          <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/pages/{{ p.id }}/{{ 'unpublish' if p.status == 'published' else 'publish' }}\">\n            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n            <button type=\"submit\">{{ 'Mark draft' if p.status == 'published' else 'Mark published' }}</button>\n          </form>\n          <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/pages/{{ p.id }}/delete\" onsubmit=\"return confirm('Delete this page?')\">\n            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n            <button type=\"submit\">Delete</button>\n          </form>\n          {% endif %}\n          {% if user[\"role\"] == \"admin\" %}\n          <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/pages/{{ p.id }}/{{ 'unlock' if p.locked else 'lock' }}\">\n            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n            <button type=\"submit\">{{ 'Unlock' if p.locked else 'Lock' }}</button>\n          </form>\n          {% endif %}\n        </td>\n      </tr>\n    {% endfor %}\n    </tbody>\n  </table>\n  {% else %}\n  <p>No pages yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n\"\"\"Admin: write, preview and manage pages.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import pages\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.web import Refused, current_user, flash, get_db, render, require_admin, verify_csrf\n\n# Every route here needs a signed-in user; every POST also needs a CSRF token.\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/pages\", dependencies=[Depends(current_user)])\ncsrf_required = [Depends(verify_csrf)]\n\n\nLOCKED_MESSAGE = (\"This page is Locked. Only the Head Coach can change it: you can read it, \"\n                  \"but not edit, publish, unpublish or delete it.\")\n\n\ndef _page_or_404(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    page = pages.get(conn, page_id)\n    if page is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No such page.\")\n    return page\n\n\ndef _changeable_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int, user) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    \"\"\"The page, if this user may change it; Locked pages are for admins only.\"\"\"\n    page = _page_or_404(conn, page_id)\n    if page[\"locked\"] and user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        raise Refused(LOCKED_MESSAGE)\n    return page\n\n\ndef _back_to_editor(page_id: int) -> RedirectResponse:\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}/edit\", status_code=303)\n\n\ndef _editor(request: Request, user, form: dict, page: sqlite3.Row | None = None,\n            status_code: int = 200, preview: str | None = None, error: str | None = None):\n    if error:\n        flash(request, error, \"error\")\n    return render(request, \"admin/page_edit.html\",\n                  {\"title\": \"Edit page\" if page else \"New page\", \"form\": form,\n                   \"page\": page, \"preview\": preview}, status_code=status_code, user=user)\n\n\ndef _slug_taken(fields: dict) -> str:\n    return f\"The slug “{fields['slug']}” is already used by another page.\"\n\n\ndef _clean(title: str, slug: str, body: str, status: str) -> tuple[dict, str | None]:\n    \"\"\"Normalise the submitted fields; return (fields, error).\"\"\"\n    title = title.strip()\n    fields = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": pages.slugify(slug or title), \"body\": body,\n              \"status\": status if status in pages.STATUSES else \"draft\"}\n    if not title:\n        return fields, \"A page needs a title.\"\n\"\"\"T05: only Matt can change Locked pages. Each test names the criterion it covers.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.routing import APIRoute\n\nfrom app import db, pages\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom app.web import require_admin\nfrom tests.conftest import _csrf_from\n\n\ndef _seed_page(database_path, title=\"Team Schedule\", slug=\"team-schedule\", locked=True):\n    conn = db.connect(database_path)\n    try:\n        page_id = pages.create(conn, title=title, slug=slug, body=\"Sat 1pm vs Denison\",\n                               status=\"published\", author_id=1)\n        if locked:\n            pages.set_locked(conn, page_id, True)\n        return page_id\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef _token(c):\n    return _csrf_from(c.get(\"/admin/pages/new\"))\n\n\ndef _save(c, page_id, **over):\n    data = {\"title\": \"Changed\", \"slug\": \"team-schedule\", \"body\": \"x\",\n            \"status\": \"published\", \"csrf_token\": _token(c)}\n    data.update(over)\n    return c.post(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef _action(c, page_id, action):\n    return c.post(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}/{action}\", data={\"csrf_token\": _token(c)},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_seeded_team_schedule_is_locked(tmp_path, monkeypatch):\n    \"\"\"The seeded Team Schedule page is Locked.\"\"\"\n    from scripts import seed_demo\n    monkeypatch.setattr(\"app.settings.DATABASE_PATH\", tmp_path / \"seed.db\")\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\", \"a-pw\")\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\", \"e-pw\")\n    assert seed_demo.main() == 0\n    conn = db.connect(tmp_path / \"seed.db\")\n    row = conn.execute(\"SELECT locked FROM pages WHERE slug = 'team-schedule'\").fetchone()\n    conn.close()\n    assert row is not None and row[\"locked\"] == 1\n\n\ndef test_admin_can_edit_publish_unpublish_delete_locked_page(client_as, database_path):\n    \"\"\"An admin can change a Locked page.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    pid = _seed_page(database_path)\n    assert _save(c, pid).status_code == 303\n    assert _action(c, pid, \"unpublish\").status_code == 303\n    assert _action(c, pid, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert _action(c, pid, \"delete\").status_code == 303\n    assert c.get(f\"/admin/pages/{pid}/edit\").status_code == 404\n\n\ndef test_admin_can_lock_and_unlock_any_page(client_as, database_path):\n    \"\"\"An admin can lock or unlock any page.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    pid = _seed_page(database_path, locked=False)\n    assert _action(c, pid, \"lock\").status_code == 303\n    conn = db.connect(database_path)\n    assert pages.get(conn, pid)[\"locked\"] == 1\n    assert _action(c, pid, \"unlock\").status_code == 303\n    assert pages.get(conn, pid)[\"locked\"] == 0\n    conn.close()\n\n\ndef test_editor_can_read_a_locked_page(client_as, database_path):\n    \"\"\"An editor can open a Locked page and see its content.\"\"\"\n    pid = _seed_page(database_path)\n    r = client_as(\"editor\").get(f\"/admin/pages/{pid}/edit\")\n    assert r.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Sat 1pm vs Denison\" in r.text\n    assert \"locked\" in r.text.lower()\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"action\", [\"save\", \"publish\", \"unpublish\", \"delete\"])\ndef test_editor_is_refused_on_a_locked_page(client_as, database_path, action):\n    \"\"\"Save, publish, unpublish and delete are refused with a clear refusal page.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    pid = _seed_page(database_path)\n    r = _save(c, pid) if action == \"save\" else _action(c, pid, action)\n    assert r.status_code == 403\n    assert \"only the head coach\" in r.text.lower()\n    conn = db.connect(database_path)\n    row = pages.get(conn, pid)\n    conn.close()\n    assert row is not None and row[\"title\"] == \"Team Schedule\" and row[\"status\"] == \"published\"\n\n\ndef test_editor_sees_no_lock_control_and_admin_does(client_as, database_path):\n    \"\"\"An editor sees no lock or unlock control.\"\"\"\n    pid = _seed_page(database_path)\n    other = _seed_page(database_path, title=\"About\", slug=\"about\", locked=False)\n    for path in (\"/admin/pages\", f\"/admin/pages/{pid}/edit\", f\"/admin/pages/{other}/edit\"):\n        editor_html = client_as(\"editor\").get(path).text\n        assert \"/unlock\" not in editor_html and f\"/{other}/lock\" not in editor_html\n    assert f\"/{pid}/unlock\" in client_as(\"admin\").get(\"/admin/pages\").text\n    assert f\"/{other}/lock\" in client_as(\"admin\").get(f\"/admin/pages/{other}/edit\").text\n\n\ndef test_forged_lock_on_unlocked_page_gets_an_honest_message(client_as, database_path):\n    \"\"\"The refusal says what happened; it does not claim the page is Locked.\"\"\"\n    other = _seed_page(database_path, title=\"About\", slug=\"about\", locked=False)\n    r = _action(client_as(\"editor\"), other, \"lock\")\n    assert r.status_code == 403 and \"is locked\" not in r.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_seed_locks_an_existing_unlocked_team_schedule(tmp_path, monkeypatch):\n    \"\"\"The seeded Team Schedule is Locked, even in a database made before T05.\"\"\"\n    from scripts import seed_demo\n    path = tmp_path / \"old.db\"\n    db.init_db(path)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(\"app.settings.DATABASE_PATH\", path)\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\", \"a-pw\")\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\", \"e-pw\")\n    seed_demo.main()\n    conn = db.connect(path)\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE pages SET locked = 0\")\n    conn.commit()\n    conn.close()\n    seed_demo.main()\n    conn = db.connect(path)\n    assert conn.execute(\"SELECT locked FROM pages WHERE slug = 'team-schedule'\").fetchone()[0] == 1\n    conn.close()\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"action\", [\"lock\", \"unlock\"])\ndef test_editor_forged_lock_change_is_refused(client_as, database_path, action):\n    \"\"\"A forged request to change the flag is refused.\"\"\"\n    pid = _seed_page(database_path)\n        return fields, \"A page needs a title.\"\n    if not fields[\"slug\"]:\n        return fields, \"The slug needs at least one letter or number.\"\n    return fields, None\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef page_list(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    return render(request, \"admin/page_list.html\",\n                  {\"title\": \"Pages\", \"pages\": pages.list_pages(conn)}, user=user)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef new_page(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    return _editor(request, user, {\"title\": \"\", \"slug\": \"\", \"body\": \"\", \"status\": \"draft\"})\n\n\n@router.post(\"/preview\", Form(\"\"),\n            user=Depends(current_user), conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    page = pages.get(conn, int(page_id)) if page_id.isdigit() else None\n    form = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body, \"status\": status}\n    return _editor(request, user, form, page, preview=render_markdown(body))\n\n\n@router.post(\"\", dependencies=csrf_required)\ndef create_page(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), slug: str = Form(\"\"),\n                body: str = Form(\"\"), status: str = Form(\"draft\"),\n                user=Depends(current_user),
